<note>The table uses medieval Arabic numerals where 4 resembles a crossed loop, 5 resembles a lowercase 'h', 7 is a caret (Λ), and 9 has a long cursive tail. Red ink (rubrication) is used for headers and the dominical letter 'a', marking Sundays. The columns provide solar altitudes (alti solis) and human shadow lengths (Umbra hois) in feet (pedes) and parts (ptes) for various times of the day (8 AM/4 PM, 9 AM/3 PM, 10 AM/2 PM).</note>

<meta>This page contains an astronomical ephemeris or calendar table calculated for a specific location. It tracks the cycles of conjunctions (Ciclo iunctis) and oppositions (Ciclo oppositio) of the sun and moon within the signs of Taurus, Gemini, and Cancer. Additionally, it records the daily position of the moon in the zodiac and provides sunrise and sunset times (ad ortum/ad occasum).</meta>

### ffor ye emposidid <gloss>at</gloss> Take fayer mayes Botter and also take

hit to geder a litel and than strayne hit and hit
is good for ye emposidid //

y bette in to poudir and meddel yt <gloss>wt</gloss> til hit be thike
and whanne it is almoost colde take poudir of gingr
and meddel yt ther wt and stere it wel to geder /

### for to make a plaſter for ye agew or for fantaſy in ye heed

Take ſowre breed maad of wheet .agood
deel of ye worthe .and take ye joes of plunntayne
in everi jote and as muche of verueyne

noryſchiþ a knaue child .and alle colde diſ-

may bi clepe ye heed for yeſe medicines
bey y prouid for treuye .

# Phlebotomy and the Zodiac Man

Aries be ware dere be ware for cottynge Gemini be ware

<margin>
Marche
Aprill
Maye
Juny
July
August
Septemb
October
Novembr
Decembr
</margin>

Taurus be ware for kuttyng i y^e necke & whils the mone be i hym

Gemini be ware for kuttynge in y^e arme & y^e shoulder & p^e vaines a-p^e for kuttyng i p^e capital veyne <unclear>p^e p^e sons ng of p^e s-</unclear>

Cancer be ware for kuttyng i the breste & y^e ribs for hurting of the stomake & longs and be ware of the herte

Leo be ware for kuttyng or hurting of p^e synewys & sides & lendes & ribis no kutte na vaine in the backe nor out of p^e sides

Virgo kutte no wonde i the wombe ne i the lowe place i y^e signe

Libra be ware for kuttynges i y^e wombe i y^e navell & i y^e lowe places nor kutte y^e vaine that renne y^e <unclear>y^t</unclear>

Scorpio be ware for kuttyng i the gendrynge members for hurting with the irons & mone be i y^e signe

Sagitarius be ware for kuttyng i y^e thyghes & synewys no kutte no wounde ne surfleyte i thos places i y^e signe

Capricornus be ware for kuttyng i the knee pappe nor of the vaines i these places ryse ne dwoe

Pisces be ware for the nat kutting ne hurt i the foot with y^e iron the mone be i y^e signe

---

->Aquarius be ware for kuttyng i the calves of y^e legges & veynes p^t go to the caffe of the ancles<-
